LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c0] ON [m].[Kind_ID] = [c0].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m0] ON [m].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m0].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [Technologies] AS [t] ON [m].[TechnologyName_ID] = [t].[ID]
WHERE LOWER([c].[EnComponentType]) = @__ToLower_0 AND LOWER([c0].[EnComponentKind]) = @__ToLower_1
